# Cantrip tracker

mtgish writes a Cantrip card file for every Magic card it parses. This page says how many of those files are complete, what the rest wait on, and which piece of Cantrip work lands the most cards. It is generated by `rust_syntax/normalize/tools/cantrip_tree.py tracker` (the layout by `tools/tracker_md.py`); do not edit it by hand. The data is in `data/cantrip_tracker.json`.

Every file is on the branch [`card-lane/generated`](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards) of vishvananda/cantrip-rs, in the crate `cantrip-generated`, written against Cantrip b12ea1c1e6. The whole crate type-checks there.

Contents: [Where things stand](#where-things-stand) · [How to read a card's file](#how-to-read-a-cards-file) · [What to build in Cantrip](#what-to-build-in-cantrip) · [Mechanics modules](#mechanics-modules) · [Proposed APIs that do not fit](#proposed-apis-that-do-not-fit) · [mtgish's own work](#mtgishs-own-work) · [Not played](#not-played) · [Reference](#reference)

## Where things stand

|  | cards | what it means |
|---|---|---|
| Magic cards | 33529 | every record mtgish parses, all faces of a card counted once |
| **whole** | **28387** | a complete file with no hole: compiles, and needs nothing Cantrip lacks (3543 of them Cantrip already has written by hand) |
| with holes | 5142 | a complete file, but some lines wait on a named requirement |
| ... written by hand in Cantrip | 123 | Cantrip already has the card; only mtgish's generator is behind |
| ... **waiting** | **5019** | what the rest of this page is about |

What each waiting card needs, counted once by the hardest thing it needs:

| fix | cards | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| generator | 4 | Cantrip can do it today; mtgish's generator has only to write it |
| mechanics module | 654 | a keyword's module in `cantrip-mechanics`, no change to the core |
| unsure | 71 | the audit could not settle whether Cantrip can do it |
| unaudited | 26 | a form that appeared after the last audit |
| mtgish data | 7 | mtgish encodes the card wrongly; fixed in mtgish, not Cantrip |
| Cantrip | 3127 | a change to Cantrip's engine or API |
| not played | 1130 | a game mode or digital-only rule Cantrip does not play |

The code inside the holes is mtgish's proposal for the API it needs. `tools/proposal_check.py` compiles it against stubs of that API under Cantrip's rules for stored data (`Copy`, `Eq`, `Hash`, no closures): of 5142 cards with holes, 2769 compile, 2298 fail only where Rust can't stub the API, and **75 propose a shape Cantrip couldn't take** ([listed below](#proposed-apis-that-do-not-fit)).

## How to read a card's file

A whole file is the card as a Cantrip PR would add it. A file with holes is the same file with each part Cantrip can't express yet wrapped in a macro (defined in `crates/cantrip-generated/src/lib.rs`):

- `planned!("id" => code)`: statements the card would run.
- `planned_value!("id", placeholder => code)`: a value; the placeholder compiles today, the code is what it will be.
- `planned_item!("id" => item)`: a whole item, such as a table or a `fn`.

The `id` is a row of [Every requirement](#every-requirement). The code inside is written as it will be once Cantrip has the feature, so landing a feature means removing the macros, not rewriting the card. When the pinned Cantrip has a feature (`src/cantrip_vocab.rs` `FEATURES`), the generator writes those cards bare.

In the tables below, **lands** is the number of waiting cards this alone stands between and a whole file; **touches** is every waiting card that needs it among other things; **written** counts cards Cantrip has already written by hand that need it, which shows a hand-written way exists. Rows are sorted by **lands**.

## Proposed APIs that do not fit

75 cards hold hole code Cantrip couldn't take in that shape. Each needs a decision in Cantrip's API before mtgish can propose it well. By what is wrong:

**a local the hole names isn't in scope** (22 cards). The hole reads a value that lives in another function or in a static table (a resolution's result, `item`, an announced X). Decide where Cantrip keeps it: a `fn` in a card table that is asked for it, or data the game state holds.

- [Apple of Eden, Isu Relic](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/apple_of_eden_isu_relic.rs): line 25, cannot find value `t_0_raw` in this scope
- [Ashiok, Dream Render](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/ashiok_dream_render.rs): line 18, cannot find value `player` in this scope
- [Back from the Brink](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/back_from_the_brink.rs): line 13, cannot find value `item` in this scope
- [Chorus of the Conclave](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/chorus_of_the_conclave.rs): line 13, cannot find value `q` in this scope
- [Commander's Plate](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/commanders_plate.rs): line 26, cannot find value `asker` in this scope
- [Demonfire](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/demonfire.rs): line 24, cannot find value `result_0` in this scope
- [Fabrication Foundry](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/fabrication_foundry.rs): line 19, cannot find value `q` in this scope

## mtgish's own work

### generator: 4 cards

Cantrip can do it today; mtgish's generator has only to write it.

| lands | touches | written | status | needs | forms | examples |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | 3 | 1 |   | A spell's choice between instruction lists asked as a labelled question; the AST carries no option labels, so `one_of` needs them from the card text or a generated label list. | `instruction-choose-one` | [Bison Whistle](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/bison_whistle.rs), [Whim of Volrath](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/whim_of_volrath.rs) |
| 1 | 1 | 0 |   | Revealing until the revealed cards satisfy a rule of the group, or a count with a limit. | `operation-reveal-until` | [Thought Dissector](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/thought_dissector.rs) |
| 0 | 3 | 1 |   | Reading what these players did to objects earlier, after the effect that did it is over | `object-collection-earlier` | [Human-Time Lord Meta-Crisis](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/human_time_lord_meta_crisis.rs), [Memory Vessel](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/memory_vessel.rs), [Triple Triad](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/triple_triad.rs) |
| 0 | 2 | 0 |   | Reading the object of a stack item a slot targets, so a rule of objects can be asked of a spell or an ability | `a-spell-or-ability-on-the-stack-read-as-an-object` | [Emissary of Grudges](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/emissary_of_grudges.rs), [Radiant Performer](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/radiant_performer.rs) |
| 0 | 2 | 0 |   | who exiled a card, with what, and when | `object-filter-exiled-by` | [Evelyn, the Covetous](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/evelyn_the_covetous.rs), [Haldan, Avid Arcanist](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/haldan_avid_arcanist.rs) |
| 0 | 1 | 0 |   | Reading the defending player of the combat under way without naming an attacker | `player-reference-defending-player` | [Nuka-Nuke Launcher](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/nuka_nuke_launcher.rs) |
| 0 | 1 | 0 |   | X announced for a spell or activation is readable while its costs are chosen and paid, not only in a target filter | `numeric-expression-announced-x` | [Droning Bureaucrats](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/droning_bureaucrats.rs) |
| 0 | 1 | 0 |   | the permanent whose effect gave an ability to its source | `object-reference-granter` | [Valki, God of Lies // Tibalt, Cosmic Impostor](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/valki_god_of_lies.rs) |
| 0 | 1 | 0 |   | which permanents an instruction just dealt damage to, as a result of that instruction | `object-filter-damaged-by` | [Huatli, Warrior Poet](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/huatli_warrior_poet.rs) |

### mtgish data: 7 cards

mtgish encodes the card wrongly; fixed in mtgish, not Cantrip.

| lands | touches | written | status | needs | forms | examples |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2 | 0 |   | a delayed or reflexive ability reads a target of the ability that made it: the item keeps those targets and each one's divided share | `target-reference` | [Redeem](https://github.com/vishvananda/cantrip-rs/blob/card-lane/generated/crates/cantrip-generated/cards/redeem.rs) |

### Counters a card brings with it

169 counter kinds are a name and nothing more (CR 122.1), used by 319 cards. They are not gaps: the card's file uses the kind, and a commit just before the card adds its line to `counter_kinds!`. The generated branch has them all in one commit.

</details>

199 tokens are made by several cards and Cantrip has no static for them; 1004 cards name them. Each is committed to `tokens.rs` before the first card that needs it, as a counter is (`tools/shared_tokens.py`).

### Text the source rewrites

mtgish changes 112 passages of card text on purpose before parsing (`grammars/find_replace.json5`). Cantrip plays Oracle text, so each has a verdict in `data/cantrip_rewrites.json`; 0 have none yet. Those that change nothing or sit on cards not played are left out.

<details>
<summary>rewrites and verdicts</summary>

| cards | verdict | note |
|---|---|---|
| Vampirism | reading | No ruling; the only reading in which "Other" does anything on an Aura. |
| Sensei Golden-Tail | undone | Oracle text says training; read.rs names the counter Training. |
| Call a Surprise Witness | reading | Rulings on Chainer and Ascent of the Worthy have the creature enter with the type; Cantrip's hand-written card adds it after. |
| Chaos Mutation | undone | One slot in Oracle text, one per player in the rewrite; target_counts.rs writes Oracle's slot. |

</details>

### Every requirement

A requirement is one form the generator could not write, not one piece of work: one feature can be many forms. `class` comes from the form's family; `fix` from `data/cantrip_audit.json`, where each form's verdict cites the API or a hand-written card. **alone** counts the cards this form alone stands between and a whole file.
